      \          3                  2023-2024                               1.16\\n\\n             4    2024-2025\
      \                               1.10\\n             5    2025-2026                               1.05\\n\\\
      n\\n\\nNote:\\n         1.  Annual  financial statements (Audited Balance sheets including  all related notes\
      \ and income\\n           statements) for last five financial years (2021-22 to 2025-26) shall be submitted\
      \ by the Tenderer.\\n\\n         2.  The financial information/Annual Turnover ( in all classes of civil engineering\
      \ construction works\\n             only) of the Tenderer must be certified either by the Independent Financial\
      \ Auditor (statutory\\n            Auditor) of the company appointed under the Companies’ Act or by a Chartered\
      \ Accountant with his\\n          stamp and signature with membership number and firm registration number.\\\
      n\\n         3.  In case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance sheet and the financial\\\
      n            information/Annual Turnover (n all classes of civil engineering construction works only) submitted,\\\
      n            the data as available in the Audited Balance sheet will be considered.\\n\\n         4.  In case\

- kind: parameter
  text: Attachment 9 specifies minimum qualification and experience requirements for key personnel, mandating a
    Project Manager (BE/B.Tech Civil with 10 years experience and 3 years in similar works) and a Chief Safety and
    Health Manager (Engineering graduate/MSc Safety with 8 years total experience and 5 years in infrastructure
    projects).

      n\\n\\n  Application for (Name of Work) ---------------------------------------\\n\\n  Tender Notification\
      \ No: ------------------------------------------------\\n\\n\\n  We, M/s ------------------ hereby apply as\
      \ a Tenderer for the above said work. In support of the application, we\\n  submit herewith untampered documents\
      \ as required. We are aware that we will be considered only if we are\\n  found to fulfil the eligibility\
      \ criteria as given in Tender document.\\n\\n We do hereby solemnly affirm and state the following:\\n\\n\
      \  1.    We understand that in the event of any information furnished by us being found later to be incorrect\\\
      n          or any material information having been suppressed, the following action can be taken in addition\
      \ to\\n          the provisions of Clause 4.33 of GCC.\\n\\n                  I. Our name may be removed from\
      \ the panel of qualified agencies.\\n\\n                 II. Any tender submitted by us based on qualification\
      \ may not be considered.\\n\\n                III. If any tender from us is accepted and a contract awarded\
      \ to us based on our qualification,      the\\n            tender acceptance may be withdrawn and the contract\
      \ awarded to us may be cancelled without\\n          any financial claim request from our side.\\n\\n  2.\
      \    We declare  that the submission  of  this Tender confirms  that no agent, middleman  or any\\n      \
      \    intermediary has been, or will be engaged to provide any services, or any other item of work related\\\
      n           to the award and performance of this Contract. We further confirm and declare that no agency\\\
      n         commission or any payment which may be construed as an agency commission has been, or will be,\\\
      n          paid and that the tender price does not include any such amount. We acknowledge the right of the\\\
      n         Employer, if he finds to the contrary, to declare our Tender to be non-compliant and if the Contract\\\
      n         has been awarded to declare the Contract null and void.\\n\\n  3.     Our Tender shall be valid\
      \ for a period as described in clause 12 of Instructions to Tenderers (ITT)\\n        and it shall remain\
      \ binding upon us and may be accepted at any time before the expiration of that\\n           period.\\n\\\
      n  4.    We understand that you are not bound to accept the lowest evaluated Tender or any other Tender\\\
      n           that you may receive.\\n\\n  5.    A Power of Attorney to sign and submit this letter is attached.\\\
      n\\n  6.    We acknowledge that the Attachments to ‘LETTER OF TECHNICAL BID’ are integral part of the\\n \
      \        Tender.\\n\\n\\n\\n  Yours faithfully,\\n  (Signature) ……………………\\n  Name of signatory: ………………………………\\\
      n  Capacity of signatory: ……………………………\\n  Name and address of Tenderer:………………..\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\

- kind: parameter
  text: Attachment 1 (Proforma Letter of Declaration and Undertaking) requires tenderers to confirm site familiarity,
    confidentiality, deployment of key personnel and plant, compliance with local content and land-border restrictions,
    and acceptance of governing laws and jurisdiction.
  evidence_ids:
  - J94f28f7a35bde32e
  - S3ad3b5d0cc707166
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: Cd040ed75e69b4102
  citations:
  - id: J94f28f7a35bde32e
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD.md
    okf_sha256: fb8297a9b0fb5a39db7af743aed04bb65c2a461730b8d1572f10654738e1a198
    heading: Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 39
    - 40
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json
    json_sha256: 6da3816d8291f7364cf6e12f62bf2c0b37ebebda9067f0516d4678f5e1f8f8d9
    json_pointer: /forms/1
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"1\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_1\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 39,\n      \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT-1\\n\\n\\n             PROFORMA LETTER OF DECLARATION AND UNDERTAKING\\\
      n\\n                             (To be submitted on Tenderer’s Letterhead)\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n     Ref:      \
      \                                                Dated……….\\n\\n\\n    The Managing Director,\\n\\n\\n   \
      \  Third floor, BMTC Complex\\n\\n   KH Road, Shanthinagar, Bangalore-560027.\\n\\n\\n    Dear Sir,\\n\\n\\\
      n    Declaration and undertaking for “(name of work) -----------------------\\n\\n    Tender Notification\
      \ No: ---------------------------------------------------\\n\\n   We M/s ------------------ for the purpose\
      \ of the Tender for the above said work, do hereby solemnly affirm\\n    and state the following as Declaration\
      \ and Undertaking:\\n\\n\\n    1.  Having inspected the site, examined the Tender document including Employer’s\
      \ requirements, General\\n        Conditions of Contract, Special Conditions of Contract, Technical Specifications,\
      \ Pricing Document,\\n          Eligibility Cum Qualification Criteria, Instructions to Tenderer and Addenda/Corrigendum\
      \ etc., thereto (if\\n       any) for the constructions of above mentioned works, and prepared the tender\
      \ entirely in accordance\\n        with all the requirements of the tender document and agree entirely with\
      \ them.\\n\\n    2. We hereby confirm that we have visited the sites of work and have become conversant with\
      \ the local\\n        conditions of working.\\n\\n    3.  For your evaluation, study, review and decision\
      \ making, we are ready to let you inspect our business\\n       premises / site, etc.\\n\\n    4. We authorize\
      \ BMRCL or any of their authorized representative to approach, enquire, verify and check\\n        the matter\
      \ furnished in our submission with the concerned client / owner of the Project / Contract and\\n        the\
      \ concerned Banker of reference provided by us.\\n\\n\\n    5. We undertake to hold in confidence all documents\
      \ and information whether Technical or commercial\\n        supplied to us at any time by or on behalf of\
      \ BMRCL in connection with this Tender without your written\\n        authority or as otherwise required by\
      \ law not to publish or otherwise disclose the same.\\n\\n\\n    6. We undertake to deploy Key Personnel \
      \ fulfilling the Qualification and Experience requirement as\\n        stipulated in Employer’s Requirements\
      \ / Tender document.\\n\\n    7.    If our Tender is accepted, we commit to deploy Plant & Machinery consistent\
      \ with the requirements\\n        stipulated in Employer’s Requirements / Tender document.\\n\\n    8.   \
      \ If our Tender is accepted, we commit to submit work method statements for all major activities and get\\\
      n       these approved from the Engineer prior to commencing work on such activities. We also understand\\\
      n         that the work shall be executed as per the approved method statements and KEY DATES  / MILE\\n \
      \    STONES without any deviations and delay in completion.\\n\\n\\n    9.    If our Tender is accepted, we\
      \ agree to establish our Project office in Bangalore.\\n\\n    10.  If our Tender is accepted, we commit to\
      \ submit a Performance Security in accordance with the Tender\\n       Documents.\"\n    },\n    {\n     \
      \ \"pdf_page\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"11. We declare that we do not have conflict of interest as described\
      \ in EQC Clause 2.13.\\n\\n      12. We declare that we have not been excluded as per the GROUNDS for EXCLUSION\
      \ mentioned in\\n         Clause 2.15 of EQC as on date of submission.\\n\\n      13. We have submitted the\
      \ Statement of Integrity, Eligibility, Social, and Environmental Responsibility\\n         singed and abide\
      \ by the same.\\n\\n\\n      14. We understand that this Tender shall be governed by and construed in all\
      \ respects according to the\\n         laws for the time being force in India. The courts at Bangalore will\
      \ have exclusive Jurisdiction in the\\n          matter.\\n\\n      15. We confirm and declare that by virtue\
      \ of our signature below, to the best of knowledge and belief that\\n          the information provided by\
      \ us as required in this Tender Document, all supporting and explanatory\\n          information is truthful\
      \ and exact.\\n\\n      16. In case our Tender is accepted, we will enter into agreement as per Clause 26.0\
      \ of Instructions to\\n         Tenderers (ITT) by signing all the contract documents mentioned in Contract\
      \ Form-1.\\n\\n      17. In case our Tender is accepted, we will comply with ‘MINIMUM LOCAL CONTENT’ in accordance\
      \ with\\n          the EQC Clause 2.3, throughout the performance of the contract. If we fail to comply with\
      \ the ‘Minimum\\n          Local Content’, Employer shall take actions in accordance with the provisions of\
      \ prevailing Public\\n         Procurement (Preference to Make in India) order issued by DPIIT-GoI and subsequent\
      \ orders issued\\n         by Govt. of India, if any, in this regard.\\n\\n\\n      18. We hereby declare\
      \ that we have read the Clause 2.10 of EQC regarding restrictions on;\\n\\n                        i.   Procurement\
      \ from a Bidder of a country which shares land borders with India.\\n\\n                        ii.    Sub-contracting\
      \ to contractors from such countries which shares land borders with India.\\n\\n                       iii.\
      \   A Bidder having Transfer of Technology (ToT) arrangement.\\n\\n\\n          Further to above mentioned\
      \ conditions we declare that;\\n\\n                        i.    the Clause 2.10 of EQC is not applicable\
      \ to us (If applicable refer ‘Note’ for compliance).\\n\\n                        ii.     in case we are awarded\
      \ the contract, we will not sub-contract any work under this contract\\n                    to a contractor\
      \ from such countries unless the contractor is registered with the competent\\n                   authority\
      \ and submits a valid registration certificate.\\n\\n                       iii.  We do not have any ToT arrangement\
      \ requiring registration with Competent Authority (If\\n                  applicable refer ‘Note’ for compliance).\\\
      n\\n\\n      19. In case of any false declarations made by us for the purpose of qualifying, we shall be disqualified\\\
      n        and our bid shall be rejected, and suitable action may be initiated in accordance with Clause 4.33\
      \ of\\n       GCC, along with such other legal actions as may be permissible under law.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n   \
      \   Yours faithfully,\\n\\n\\n       (Signature) ……………………\\n\\n     Name of signatory: ………………………………\\n\\n\
      \      Capacity of signatory: ……………………………\\n\\n     Name and address of Tenderer:……………….\\n\\n\\n        \
      \ Note; In case the bidder is sharing land border with India and if the Clause 2.10 is applicable, then\\\
      n          bidder is required to register with the Competent Authority as per Clause 2.10 and he shall submit\\\
      n          the copy of the same along with tender submission.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"ATTACHMENT-1\"\
      ,\n  \"kind\": \"attachment\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"source_marking\": \"\
      not_marked_deleted\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified

  - id: S3ad3b5d0cc707166
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD.md
    okf_sha256: fb8297a9b0fb5a39db7af743aed04bb65c2a461730b8d1572f10654738e1a198
    heading: Attachment-1 — Proforma Letter of Declaration and Undertaking
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    pdf_pages:
    - 39
    - 40
    quote: '- **Tender Document and Site Familiarity** (Items 1–2)

      - **Inspection and Verification Authorisation** (Items 3–4)

      - **Confidentiality Undertaking** (Item 5)

      - **Key Personnel Deployment** (Item 6)

      - **Plant and Machinery Deployment** (Item 7)

      - **Work Method Statements and Key Dates** (Item 8)

      - **Project Office in Bangalore** (Item 9)

      - **Performance Security Commitment** (Item 10)

      - **Conflict of Interest Declaration** (Item 11; EQC Clause 2.13)

      - **Grounds for Exclusion Declaration** (Item 12; EQC Clause 2.15)

      - **Statement of Integrity Submission** (Item 13; Attachment-16)

      - **Governing Law and Jurisdiction** (Item 14)

      - **Truthfulness of Information** (Item 15)

      - **Contract Agreement Commitment** (Item 16; ITT Clause 26.0; Contract Form-1)

      - **Minimum Local Content Commitment** (Item 17; EQC Clause 2.3)

      - **Land-Border Restrictions Declaration** (Item 18; EQC Clause 2.10)

      - **Consequences of False Declaration** (Item 19; GCC Clause 4.33)

      - **Land-Border Registration Note** (Note; EQC Clause 2.10)'
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference

- kind: parameter
  text: Attachment 1A mandates that tenderers willing to undertake multiple contracts specify concurrent tender
    numbers, acknowledging a maximum award limit of two contracts and committing to separate teams and equipment.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jc8e3c220abf05a5f
  - S923afd7933dcab85
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

  - id: Jc8e3c220abf05a5f
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD.md
    okf_sha256: fb8297a9b0fb5a39db7af743aed04bb65c2a461730b8d1572f10654738e1a198

    json_pointer: /forms/2
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"1A\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_1a\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 41,\n      \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -1A\\n\\n\\n            UNDERTAKING FOR EXECUTION OF MULTIPLE\
      \ WORK CONTRACTS\\n\\n                                     (Refer Clause 22.4.2 of ITT)\\n\\n   (TO BE SUBMITTED\
      \ ON TENDERER’S LETTERHEAD BY THE BIDDER WHO IS WILLING TO\\n                       UNDERTAKE MULTIPLE CONTRACTS)\\\
      n\\n       Ref:                                                      Dated……….\\n\\n     The Managing Director,\\\
      n       Third floor, BMTC Complex\\n     KH Road, Shanthinagar, Bangalore-560027.\\n\\n      Dear Sir,\\n\\\
      n       I/We, ……….. [Tenderer's Name], hereby declare that we are also submitting Bids for the following\\\
      n       other tenders (works) which are invited concurrently along with this tender, and we understand\\n\
      \        that we shall be awarded a maximum of Two (2) contracts only.\\n\\n         Sl.No.              \
      \       Tender Number as per 2.3 of ITT\\n         1\\n         2\\n         3\\n\\n\\n       I/We, ………..\
      \ [Tenderer's Name], hereby confirm that  if any Two contracts are awarded as per\\n      ITT Clause 23.2\
      \ among the Contracts for which we have submitted Bids, we undertake to execute\\n       both the awarded\
      \ Contracts  in accordance with the terms and conditions of the respective\\n       contracts and we also\
      \ undertake to deploy Separate Teams and Separate set of Plant &\\n      equipment for each contract.\\n\\\
      n    We hereby declare that we have carefully reviewed the requirements of the tender documents and\\n   \
      \   understand the scope of work, timelines, and obligations for each work. We confirm that we have\\n   \
      \    the necessary resources, expertise, and capacity to execute simultaneously both work contracts if\\n\
      \      awarded to us.\\n\\n    We acknowledge that the Employer reserves the right to award contracts based\
      \ on the most cost-\\n        effective and beneficial combination of works to the Employer.\\n\\n    We certify\
      \ that the information provided in this undertaking is true and accurate to the best of our\\n      knowledge.\\\
      n\\n\\n       Authorized Signature: __________\\n     Name: _____________\\n       Designation: ____________\\\
      n       Date: _____________\\n\\n      Note:\\n            1.  Submission of this Attachment-1A is mandatory\
      \ for Tenderers who seek to be\\n             considered for the award of more than one contract and shall\
      \ mention the Tender\\n               Notification Nos of other tenders in which they intend to participate\
      \ (Max. Two will\\n            be awarded).\\n            2.  Tenderers seeking the award of only one contract\
      \ are not required to submit this\\n             Attachment-1A.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"ATTACHMENT\
      \ -1A\",\n  \"kind\": \"attachment\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"source_marking\"\
      : \"not_marked_deleted\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified

    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf

    - 41
    quote: '- **Applicability** (Refer ITT Clause 22.4.2)

      - **Declaration of Concurrent Tenders** (Tender numbers per ITT Clause 2.3)

      - **Maximum Two Contracts**

      - **Separate Teams and Equipment Undertaking** (ITT Clause 23.2)

      - **Capacity Confirmation**

      - **Employer''s Right to Award Most Beneficial Combination**

      - **Submission Notes** (Notes 1–2)'

    quote: "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"6\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_6\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"\

    and 10 years experience, and a Chief Safety and Health Manager with an Engineering degree or MSc in Safety and
    8 years total experience.

- kind: parameter
  text: Attachment 10 mandates minimum plants and equipment to be deployed, including diamond disc cutters, wall
    sawing machines, electric drill motors, floor saws, chipping machines, crushers, hydraulic mobile cranes, long
    reach demolition equipment, rock breakers, and DG sets.

    quote: "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"10\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_10\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 52,\n      \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -10\\n\\n                                   (To be submitted\

      \             (TO BE DEPLOYED AS AND WHEN REQUIRED)\\n\\n\\n       Tenderers should have the following plant\
      \ and machinery either owned or leased or hired by him.\\n\\n        Sl.\\n              Type of equipment\
      \ required for the work         Proposed to be deployed\\n\\n\\n       1    Diamond  disc  Cutter/floor saw\
      \  machine  with               3 Nos\\n               suitable capacity\\n\\n       2    Wall Sawing Machine\
      \ with suitable capacity                    3 Nos\\n\\n       3     Electric Drill Motors/ Hammer drilling\
      \ machine                  4 Nos\\n\\n\\n       4     Floor Saw Machine with suitable capacity           \
      \           4 Nos\\n\\n       5    Chipping Machine                                        12 Nos\\n\\n  \
      \     6    Crusher/Combi Crusher                                    2 Nos\\n\\n       7     Hydraulic Mobile\
      \ crane                                     2 Nos\\n\\n       8    High/Long Reach Demolition equipment as\
      \ per               1 Nos\\n              the requirement\\n\\n       9     Hydraulic rock breaker with loader\
      \                            2 Nos\\n\\n      10  DG set 100kV or higher capacity                        \
      \     2 Nos\\n\\n\\n  NOTE:\\n        1.  Plant and Equipment indicated above is minimum to be deployed at\
      \ appropriate stage of the work.\\n          However, depending on the requirement to complete the work in\
      \ the stipulated completion period,\\n           the tenderer should deploy additional machinery as circumstances\
      \ warrant at no extra cost.\\n        2.  Plant and equipment to be mobilized for the work shall be in good\
      \ serviceable condition.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nSignature) ……………………\\nName of signatory: ………………………………\\nCapacity\
      \ of signatory: ……………………………\\nName and address of Tenderer:………………..\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"\

- kind: parameter
  text: Attachment 12 and Attachment 12A provide the prescribed formats for Bank Guarantees for Tender Security
    (EMD) and Surety Bonds for Bid Security, specifying BMRCL's bank beneficiary details at the Bank of India Cantonment
    Branch.

    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD.md
    okf_sha256: fb8297a9b0fb5a39db7af743aed04bb65c2a461730b8d1572f10654738e1a198
    heading: Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c

    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json

    json_record_id: null

    quote: "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"12\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_12\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\\n\\n\\n      FORM OF BANK GUARANTEE FOR TENDER SECURITY/\
      \ EARNEST MONEY DEPOSIT\\n\\n                       (To be stamped in accordance with the Stamp Act of the\
      \ Country of Issuing Bank)\\n\\n     To,\\n    Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\\n      3rd\
      \ Floor BMTC Complex,\\n     K.H. Road, Shanthinagar\\n     Bengaluru- 560027.\\n     Karnataka, INDIA.\\\
      n\\n1.  WHEREAS ______________ [Name and Address of the Tenderer] (hereinafter referred to as the “Bidder”\
      \ or\\n     “Tenderer”) has  undertaken  for  submission  of  Tender  in  pursuance  of  Tender  Notification\
      \  No.\\n    _____________ for _________________________ [Name of the Works] (hereinafter referred to as the\
      \ “Bid”\\n     or “Tender”).\\n\\n2.  AND WHEREAS it has been stipulated in the Tender issued by Bangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Limited\\n    (BMRCL) (hereinafter referred to as the “Employer” or “BMRCL”) that\
      \ the Tenderer shall furnish to\\n    Employer an unconditional and irrevocable Bank Guarantee from an Indian\
      \ Scheduled Bank (excluding\\n     Cooperative Bank) or from a Scheduled Foreign Bank as defined in Section\
      \ 2(e) of RBI Act 1934 read with\\n    second schedule, for the sum specified herein below as Tender Security(EMD)\
      \ for compliance of the\\n     obligations in accordance with the Tender conditions. In pursuance to this,\
      \ M/s_________ has requested\\n      for issuance of the Bank Guarantee for the sum specified herein below\
      \ as Tender security (EMD).\\n\\n\\n3.  AND WHEREAS we _____________________________ [Name and full address\
      \ of the Bank including Email\\n     address] having registered office at ____________________________ (hereinafter\
      \ referred to as the\\n     “Bank”), which expression shall unless repugnant to the context or the meaning\
      \ thereof, include  its\\n     successors, administrators, executors, assignees, do hereby agree to give the\
      \ Employer such a Bank\\n    Guarantee drawn and payable at Bengaluru ________ branch on behalf of the tenderer.\\\
      n\\n\\n4.  NOW therefore, the Bank hereby affirm that  it is the guarantor and responsible to ‘Employer’ and\
      \ their\\n     successors,  on  behalf   of  the  ‘Tenderer’  up  to  a   total  sum   of  INR/Foreign  Currency/ies\\\
      n    _________________________________ [the amount of Guarantee to be specified in figure and words in the\\\
      n     respective currency/ies as  specified  in  the Tender document]. Further, the Bank hereby unconditionally,\\\
      n     irrevocably and without demur undertake to immediately pay, the Employer upon the Employer’s  first\\\
      n     written demand and without cavil, or arguments any sum or sums within the limits of the sum specified\
      \ in\\n      this Bank Guarantee as aforesaid without the Employer being required to prove or to show grounds\
      \ or\\n    reasons for the demand, by depositing/crediting to the Current Account of the Employer, the sums\\\
      n     specified herein.\\n\\n\\n5.   This Bank Guarantee is encashable at our designated Branch as mentioned\
      \ below:\\n\\n    Name of the Bank, Branch and contact details Address Telephone no: & Fax No: E-mail Address\
      \ Branch Manager/s\\n    name & mobile No: Bank Zonal Office Address, Telephone No, Fax Number, E-mail Address:\
      \ __________________\\n                                   OR\\n     This e- Bank Guarantee shall be encashable\
      \ at the issuing branch of the Bank through NeSL portal.\\n6.  The Bank hereby confirms that the Bank is on\
      \ the SFMS (Structural Finance Messaging System) platform\\n    and shall invariably send an advice of the\
      \ Bank Guarantee to the designated Bank of the Employer as\\n     given below:\\n\\n         Sl. No.  Particulars\
      \              Details\\n                i      Name of Beneficiary     Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited\\\
      n                ii      Name of Bank         Bank of India\\n                iii       Branch           \
      \    Cantonment Branch, Bengaluru.\\n          iv       Address of Branch       49, Jyothi Mahal,\\n     \
      \                                           St. Mark’s Road, Bangalore - 560001\\n       v       Account No.\
      \           840420110000760\\n          vi      IFSC Code:           BKID0008404\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"7.   This Bank Guarantee shall be encashed at the demand of the Employer\
      \ on or before the due date.\\n\\n8.  The Employer shall have full rights to encash this bank guarantee at\
      \ any time during the validity period and\\n     the ‘Tenderer’ shall have no right or claim whatsoever in\
      \ the matter of encashment of the Bank guarantee\\n    amount by the ‘Employer’. The Bank will be fully responsible\
      \ in terms of the guarantee and obligation, to\\n   make immediate payment to ‘Employer’, without the consent\
      \ of the Tenderer and without referring the\\n     matter to the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n9.  The Bank will deliver\
      \ the money required by the ‘Employer’ immediately on demand without delay without\\n     reference to the\
      \ ‘Tenderer’ and without the necessity of a previous notice or of judicial or administrative\\n    procedures\
      \ and without it being necessary to prove to the Bank the liability or damages resulting from any\\n     defects\
      \ or shortcomings or debts of the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n10.  Courts at Bengaluru, Karnataka, India shall have exclusive\
      \ jurisdiction for adjudicating the disputes arising\\n     out of the encashment of the Guarantee, and, the\
      \ said Bank undertakes not to revoke this Guarantee\\n     during its currency, except with the previous consent\
      \ of the ‘Employer’ in writing and agree that any change\\n      in the constitution of the Bank shall not\
      \ discharge its liability hereunder.\\n\\n11.  This Bank Guarantee will not be discharged due to the change\
      \ in the constitution of the ‘Tenderer’ or\\n    change in the constitution of the bank.\\n\\n12.  This Deed\
      \ of guarantee is signed and executed by Shri/Smt ______________ and Shri/Smt ______ who\\n     is/are authorized\
      \ by the Bank for issuing the guarantee on  its behalf and their ID No/s. and Email\\n    address/es being________\
      \ & ________ and ________ & ________ respectively.\\n\\n13. The Bank’s liability under this Guarantee shall\
      \ not exceed the amount of ______ [Indian Rupees/Foreign\\n     Currency/ies to be specified in words and\
      \ figures].\\n\\n14.  This guarantee shall be valid till _________ [Date to be mentioned].\\n\\n15. The pendency\
      \ of any dispute or legal proceedings shall not affect this guarantee in any manner.\\n\\n16.   It is hereby\
      \ agreed that the liability of the Bank under this guarantee shall cease on whichever of the\\n     following\
      \ events first occurs.\\n        a) Payment by the Bank of the guaranteed sum in full to the ‘Employer’,\\\
      n                                                 or\\n        b) Receipt of written communication from the\
      \ ‘Employer’ to the Bank along with the   original Bank\\n        Guarantee discharging the Bank of  its \
      \ liability covered by this Bank Guarantee and in case of\\n          electronic Bank Guarantee (e-BG), after\
      \ release/discharge of e-BG by the Employer through NeSL\\n           portal.\\n\\n                      \
      \      SIGNATURE AND SEAL OF THE GUARANTOR\\n\\n                               DATE: ___________________\\\
      n                         NAME OF THE BANK: ______________________________\\n                            ADDRESS:\
      \ _______________________________________\\nNote:\\n1.  The stamp paper of appropriate value shall be purchased\
      \ in the name of the bank, who issues the “Bank\\n    Guarantee”.\\n2.  The above format shall be used for\
      \ e-Bank Guarantee also.\\n3.   For issuance of e-BG, the UIN (PAN No.) of BMRCL is AAACB4881D.\\n4.  Tender\
      \ No. as mentioned in relevant para of ITT pertaining to Tender Security shall be the reference to be\\n \
      \   mentioned in the e-BG.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\",\n  \"kind\": \"attachment\"\
      ,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"source_marking\": \"not_marked_deleted\"\n}"

      \            S.B. No.                                                                 Dated:\\n\\n\\n    \
      \       1.  In consideration of you, Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation, having its office at B.M.T.C Complex,\\\

      n              referred to as \\\"the Project\\\") pursuant to the Tender Document issued in respect of the\
      \ Project\\n           and other related documents including without limitation the draft contract agreement\
      \ (hereinafter\\n               collectively referred to as \\\"Bidding Documents\\\"), we (Name of the Surety\
      \ Insurer) having our\\n              registered office at............... and one of its branches at.................\
      \ (hereinafter referred to as the\\n             \\\"Surety Insurer \\\"), at the request of the Bidder, do\
      \ hereby in terms of Clause 13 of ITT of Bid\\n            Document, irrevocably, unconditionally and without\
      \ reservation guarantee the due and faithful\\n                fulfilment and compliance of the terms and\
      \ conditions of the Bidding Documents by the said\\n             Bidder and  unconditionally and  irrevocably\
      \ undertake  to pay  forthwith  to  the Authority an\\n           amount  of  Rs. …………... (hereinafter referred\
      \  to as the \\\"Surety Bond\\\") as our primary\\n               obligation without any demur, reservation,\
      \ recourse, contest or protest and without reference to\\n             the Bidder,  if the Bidder shall fail\
      \ to fulfil or comply with all or any of the terms and conditions\\n             contained in the said Bidding\
      \ Documents.\\n\\n           2.  Any such written demand made by the Authority stating that the Bidder is\
      \ in default of the due\\n           and faithful fulfilment and compliance with the terms and conditions\
      \ contained in the Bidding\\n           Documents shall be final, conclusive and binding on the Surety Insurer.\\\
      n\\n\\n           3.  We, the Surety Insurer, do hereby unconditionally undertake to pay the amounts due and\
      \ payable\\n            under this Surety Bond without any demur, reservation, recourse, contest or protest\
      \ and without\\n            any reference to the Bidder or any other person and irrespective of whether the\
      \ claim of the\\n              Authority is disputed by the Bidder or not, merely on the first demand from\
      \ the Authority stating\\n               that the amount claimed is due to the Authority by reason of failure\
      \ of the Bidder to  fulfil and\\n            comply with the terms and conditions contained in the Bidding\
      \ Documents including failure of\\n             the said Bidder to keep its Bid open during the Bid validity\
      \ period as set forth in the said Bidding\\n           Documents for any reason whatsoever. Any such demand\
      \ made on the Bank shall be conclusive\\n            as regards amount due and payable by the Surety Insurer\
      \ under this Surety Bond. However, our\\n                   liability under this Surety Bond shall be restricted\
      \ to an amount not exceeding Rs……\\n\\n\\n           4.  This Surety Bond shall be irrevocable and remain\
      \ in full force for a period of 240 days from the\\n             Bid Due Date inclusive of a claim period\
      \ of 60 days or  for such extended period as may\\n           be mutually agreed between the Authority and\
      \ the Bidder, and agreed to by the Surety Insurer,\\n           and shall continue to be enforceable till\
      \ all amounts under this Surety Bond have been paid.\\n\\n           5.  We, the Surety Insurer, further agree\
      \ that the Authority shall be the sole judge to decide as to\\n            whether the Bidder is in default\
      \ of due and faithful fulfilment and compliance with the terms and\\n              conditions contained in\
      \ the Bidding Documents including, Inter alia, the failure of the Bidder to\\n            keep its Bid open\
      \ during the Bid validity period set forth in the said Bidding Documents, and the\\n              decision\
      \ of the Authority that the Bidder is in default as aforesaid shall be final and binding on\\n           \
      \   us, notwithstanding any differences between the  Authority and  the  Bidder  or  any  dispute\\n     \
      \       pending before any Court, Tribunal, Arbitrator or any other Authority.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 57,\n      \"text\": \"6.  The Surety Bond shall not be affected by any change in the constitution\
      \ or winding up of the\\n             Bidder or the Surety Insurer or any absorption, merger or amalgamation\
      \ of the Bidder or the\\n             Surety Insurer with any other person.\\n\\n           7.  To give full\
      \ effect to this Surety Bond, the Authority shall be entitled to treat the Surety Insurer as\\n          \
      \   the  principal debtor. The Authority shall have the  fullest  liberty without affecting in any way\\n\
      \             the liability of the Surety Insurer under this Surety Bond from time to time to vary any of\
      \ the\\n            terms and conditions contained in the said Bidding Documents or to extend time for submission\
      \ of\\n             the Bids or the Bid validity period or the period for conveying acceptance of Letter of\
      \ Award by\\n             the Bidder or the period for fulfilment and compliance with all or any of the terms\
      \ and conditions\\n             contained in the said Bidding Documents by the said Bidder or to postpone\
      \ for any time and from\\n             time to time any of the powers exercisable by it against the said Bidder\
      \ and either to enforce or\\n              forbear from enforcing any of the terms and conditions contained\
      \ in the said Bidding Documents\\n              or the securities available to the Authority, and the Surety\
      \ Insurer shall not be released from its\\n                  liability under these presents by any exercise\
      \ by the Authority of the liberty with reference to the\\n             matters aforesaid or by reason of time\
      \ being given to the said Bidder or any other forbearance,\\n              act or omission on the part of\
      \ the Authority or any indulgence by the Authority to the said Bidder\\n              or by any change in\
      \ the constitution of the Authority or its absorption, merger or amalgamation\\n              with any other\
      \ person or any other matter or thing whatsoever which under the law relating to\\n              sureties\
      \ would but for this provision have the effect of releasing the Surety Insurer from its such\\n          \
      \         liability.\\n\\n           8.  Any notice by way of request, demand or otherwise hereunder shall\
      \ be sufficiently given in\\n               writing or made if addressed to the Surety Insurer and sent by\
      \ courier or by registered post or by\\n                certified e-mail to the Surety Insurer at the address\
      \ or e-mail set forth herein.\\n\\n           9. We undertake to make the payment on receipt of your notice\
      \ of claim on us addressed to name of\\n             Surety Insurer along with branch address and delivered\
      \ at our above branch who shall be\\n          deemed to have been duly authorized to receive the said notice\
      \ of claim.\\n\\n         10.   It  shall  not be necessary  for  the  Authority  to proceed  against  the\
      \  said  Bidder  before\\n             proceeding against the Surety Insurer and the Surety Bond herein contained\
      \ shall be enforceable\\n             against the Surety Insurer, notwithstanding any other security which\
      \ the Authority may have\\n             obtained from the said Bidder or any other person and which shall,\
      \ at the time when proceedings\\n             are taken against the Surety Insurer hereunder, be outstanding\
      \ or unrealized.\\n\\n         11. We, the Surety Insurer, further undertake not to revoke this Surety Bond\
      \ during  its currency\\n             except with the previous express consent of the Authority in writing.\\\
      n\\n         12. The Surety Insurer declares that  it has power to issue this Surety Bond and discharge the\\\
      n              obligations contemplated herein, the undersigned  is duly authorised and has  full power to\\\
      n            execute this Surety Bond for and on behalf of the Surety Insurer.\\n\\n         13. For the avoidance\
      \ of doubt, the Surety Insurer's liability under this Surety Bond shall be restricted\\n               to\
      \ Rs. ………….. The Surety Insurer shall be liable to pay the said amount or any part thereof\\n            \
      \ only if the Authority serves a written claim on the Surety insurer in accordance with paragraph 9\\n   \
      \           hereof, on or before [*** (indicate date falling 240 days after the Bid Due Date)].\\n\\n\\n \
      \        14. This Surety Bond  shall also be operatable at our ………………….., branch  in India, from\\n      \
      \    whom, confirmation regarding the issue of this Surety Bond or extension/renewal thereof shall be\\n \
      \         made available on demand. In the contingency of this Surety Bond being invoked and payment\\n  \
      \          hereunder claimed, the said branch  shall accept such invocation letter and make payment of\\n\
      \           amounts so demanded under the said invocation.\\n\\n         15. The Insurance Surety Bond shall\
      \ be verified from the specific portal created for this purpose.\\n\\n Signed and delivered by ………………..Company\\\
      n\\n\\n\\n By the hand of Mr./Ms.....it’s... and authorized official\\n\\n                               \
      \                        (Signature of the Authorized Signatory) (Official Seal)\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
      : \"ATTACHMENT -12A\",\n  \"kind\": \"attachment\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"\
      source_marking\": \"not_marked_deleted\"\n}"

    member, including percentage participation; project, client, commencement/completion and extension details;
    award value and completed/substantially completed contract value excluding GST as at the last day of the month
    before tender invitation; and updated completed-contract value excluding GST using Attachment 6 inflation factors.
    These are requested particulars, not completed bidder entries.

- kind: summary
  text: Attachment 11 is the Power of Attorney template for bidding, referring to ITT 14.1. It specifies non-judicial
    stamp paper of appropriate value in accordance with the stamp act if in India, or an equivalent document duly
    attested by a notary public. The tenderer authorizes its named employee to act on the tenderer’s behalf in bid
    signing, submission, meetings and dealings with BMRCL. The execution notes refer to applicable law, charter
    procedures and supporting authority documents where required; the template does not appoint that employee to
    represent BMRCL.

# Letter of Technical Bid Attachments Overview

> Draft model-derived knowledge. Citation validation establishes supplied-source provenance, not interpretation correctness.

## Summary

- Technical Volume 1 Section D specifies the required attachments to the Letter of Technical Bid, including work experience summaries, ongoing works commitments, financial turnover, key personnel qualifications, plant and equipment, and administrative forms.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 65; quotation `J54cce335d71fa4b6`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/submission_checklist.json#/full_form_context/0`.

- Technical Volume 1 Section D defines the Letter of Technical Bid and its accompanying numbered attachments, detailing declarations, undertakings, and qualification information required from tenderers.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37; quotation `J35c09e1fb40d1a04`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/contents_page`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 38; quotation `J92b9df54ae44bed3`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/0`.

- Several prescribed attachments in Section D are explicitly marked as DELETED in the tender documents, including Attachment 2, Attachment 3, Attachment 3A, Attachment 3B, Attachment 13A, and Attachment 15.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 42; quotation `J0a2b34fa4fe5f8a8`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/3`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 42; quotation `J117384e47a4b1ce3`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/4`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37; quotation `J35c09e1fb40d1a04`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/contents_page`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 42; quotation `J51d4e5c747f31e55`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/5`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 42; quotation `Jaaf3958372f3a0c4`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/6`.

- Technical Volume 1 Section D enumerates the required and deleted attachments accompanying the Letter of Technical Bid, including administrative forms, qualification proofs, financial statements, and technical submittals.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 37; quotation `J35c09e1fb40d1a04`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/contents_page`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 65; quotation `J54cce335d71fa4b6`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/submission_checklist.json#/full_form_context/0`.

- Tenderers are required under Attachment 7 to report on their current commitments, awarded contracts, letters of intent or acceptance received, and contracts approaching completion for which completion certificates are still pending.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 47; quotation `J3a1588c3042057c9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/attachment_7_tables.json#/notes/1`.

- Attachment 8 mandates the submission of annual financial statements, including audited balance sheets with related notes and income statements, covering the last five financial years from 2021-22 to 2025-26.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 49; quotation `J53dbfdb2f9542fe9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/attachment_8_tables.json#/notes/0`.

- Attachment 9 establishes the minimum qualification and experience requirements for key personnel deployed on the works, specifying mandatory credentials and post-qualification experience for the Project Manager and Chief Safety and Health Manager.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 50; quotation `Je29647f69b1560f9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/attachment_9_tables.json#/full_form_context/0`.

- Attachment 4 sets out the standard Tenderer's Information form, requiring legal name, address, date of incorporation, authorized representative details, bank details including account number, IFSC code, PAN, GST registration number, copy of cancelled cheque, and articles of incorporation or constitution.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 43; quotation `Jccd4f83626ee3522`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/7`.

- Attachment 5 requires a separate form for each similar work, with the relevant client certificate enclosed. The template asks whether the work was carried out as prime contractor acting as a single entity or JV/consortium member, including percentage participation; project, client, commencement/completion and extension details; award value and completed/substantially completed contract value excluding GST as at the last day of the month before tender invitation; and updated completed-contract value excluding GST using Attachment 6 inflation factors. These are requested particulars, not completed bidder entries.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 45; quotation `Jd2d184711db13a7f`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/9`.

- Attachment 9A prescribes the curriculum vitae (CV) format for proposed key professional staff, capturing proposed position, firm name, staff name, profession, date of birth, years with firm, nationality, professional memberships, detailed assigned tasks, key qualifications, education, employment record with reverse chronological periods and client references, language proficiency, and certification signed by staff and authorized representative.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 51; quotation `J427fda3fc68c546c`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/14`.

- Attachment 11 is the Power of Attorney template for bidding, referring to ITT 14.1. It specifies non-judicial stamp paper of appropriate value in accordance with the stamp act if in India, or an equivalent document duly attested by a notary public. The tenderer authorizes its named employee to act on the tenderer’s behalf in bid signing, submission, meetings and dealings with BMRCL. The execution notes refer to applicable law, charter procedures and supporting authority documents where required; the template does not appoint that employee to represent BMRCL.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 53; quotation `Jccd2341b74f6e498`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json#/forms/16`.

- Key personnel requirements under Attachment 9 specify a Project Manager holding a BE or B.Tech in Civil Engineering with 10 years of post-qualification experience, including 3 years as a Project Manager or equivalent in similar nature works, with a minimum required quantity of 1.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 50; quotation `Je29647f69b1560f9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/attachment_9_tables.json#/full_form_context/0`.

- Key personnel requirements under Attachment 9 specify a Chief Safety and Health Manager holding an Engineering degree or an MSc in Safety from a recognized university with 8 years of total post-qualification experience, including 5 years as a Chief Safety and Health Manager or equivalent in infrastructure projects, with a minimum required quantity of 1.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 50; quotation `Je29647f69b1560f9`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/attachment_9_tables.json#/full_form_context/0`.

The financial information/Annual Turnover ( in all classes of civil engineering construction works\n             only) of the Tenderer must be certified either by the Independent Financial Auditor (statutory\n            Auditor) of the company appointed under the Companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with his\n          stamp and signature with membership number and firm registration number."

>   "text": "5.  To align a January–December financial year with the standard April–March fiscal year, use the\n             following conversion:\n\n        A calendar year (e.g., January–December 2023) corresponds to the subsequent fiscal year (e.g.,\n         FY 2023–24, covering  April 2023  to March 2024). Apply  this  rule  consistently  to  all such\n            conversions."
> }

### J92b9df54ae44bed3

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]

> {

>     {
>       "pdf_page": 38,
>       "text": "LETTER OF TECHNICAL BID\n\n                               (To be submitted on Tenderer’s Letterhead)\n\n\n\n  Ref:                                                  Dated……….\n\n  The Managing Director,\n   Third floor, BMTC Complex\n  KH Road, Shanthinagar, Bangalore-560027.\n\n  Dear Sir,\n\n\n  Application for (Name of Work) ---------------------------------------\n\n  Tender Notification No: ------------------------------------------------\n\n\n  We, M/s ------------------ hereby apply as a Tenderer for the above said work. In support of the application, we\n  submit herewith untampered documents as required. We are aware that we will be considered only if we are\n  found to fulfil the eligibility criteria as given in Tender document.\n\n We do hereby solemnly affirm and state the following:\n\n  1. Our name may be removed from the panel of qualified agencies.\n\n                 II. Any tender submitted by us based on qualification may not be considered.\n\n                III. If any tender from us is accepted and a contract awarded to us based on our qualification,      the\n            tender acceptance may be withdrawn and the contract awarded to us may be cancelled without\n          any financial claim request from our side.\n\n  2.    We declare  that the submission  of  this Tender confirms  that no agent, middleman  or any\n          intermediary has been, or will be engaged to provide any services, or any other item of work related\n           to the award and performance of this Contract. We acknowledge the right of the\n         Employer, if he finds to the contrary, to declare our Tender to be non-compliant and if the Contract\n         has been awarded to declare the Contract null and void.\n\n  3. We understand that you are not bound to accept the lowest evaluated Tender or any other Tender\n           that you may receive.\n\n  5.    A Power of Attorney to sign and submit this letter is attached.\n\n  6.    We acknowledge that the Attachments to ‘LETTER OF TECHNICAL BID’ are integral part of the\n         Tender.\n\n\n\n  Yours faithfully,\n  (Signature) ……………………\n  Name of signatory: ………………………………\n  Capacity of signatory: ……………………………\n  Name and address of Tenderer:……………….."
